{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}% latex2html id marker 305
a_{j}(t+1) = f_{act}(net_{j}(t), a_{j}(t), \theta_{j})
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}% latex2html id marker 312
{ll}
         $a_{j}(t)$   & activation of unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $net_{j}(t)$ & net input in unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $\theta_{j}$ & threshold (bias) of unit $j$\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$f_{act}(x) = 1 / (1+e^{-x})$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8972: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$(t+1)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8974: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$[0,1]$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8976: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$]0,1[$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8978: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{% latex2html id marker 8980
$\theta_{j}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8980: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$net_{j}(t)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline8984: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}net_{j}(t) & = & \sum_{i} w_{ij} o_{i}(t) \quad 
        \mbox{if unit $j$ has no sites } \\ 
        net_{j}(t) & = & \sum_{k} s_{jk}(t)       \quad 
        \mbox{if the unit $j$ has sites, with site values}\\ 
        s_{jk}(t) & = & \sum_{i} w_{ij} o_{i}(t) \nonumber\\ 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}% latex2html id marker 341
a_{j}(t+1) = \frac{1}{{1 + e^{- \left( \sum_{i} w_{ij} o_{i}(t) 
                                        - \theta_{j} \right) }}}
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}% latex2html id marker 349
{ll}
         $a_{j}(t)$   & activation of unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $net_{j}(t)$ & net input in unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $o_{i}(t)$   & output of unit $i$ in step $t$\\ 
         $s_{jk}(t)$  & site value of site $k$ on unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $j$          & index for some unit in the net\\ 
         $i$          & index of a predecessor of the unit $j$\\ 
         $k$          & index of a site of unit $j$\\ 
         $w_{ij}$     & weight of the link from unit $i$ to unit $j$\\ 
         $\theta_{j}$ & threshold (bias) of unit $j$\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}o_{j}(t) = f_{out}(a_{j}(t))
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
         $a_{j}(t)$ &    Activation of unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $o_{j}(t)$ &    Output of unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
         $j$        &    Index for all units of the net\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$[0..1]$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9050: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}o_{j}(t) = \left\{ 
        \begin{array}{ll}
        0 & \mbox{if $a_{j}(t) < 0$}\\ 
        1 & \mbox{if $a_{j}(t) > 1$}\\ 
        a_{j}(t) & \mbox{otherwise}\\ 
        \end{array}
        \right.
\end{eqnarray*}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
\stepcounter{subsection}
\stepcounter{section}
\stepcounter{section}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\Delta w_{ij} = g(a_{j}(t), t_{j}) h(o_{i}(t),w_{ij})
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $w_{ij}$     & weight of the link from unit $i$ to unit $j$\\ 
  $a_{j}(t)$   & activation of unit $j$ in step $t$\\ 
  $t_{j}$      & teaching input, in general the desired output of unit $j$\\  
  $o_{i}(t)$   & output of unit $i$ at time $t$\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $g(\dots)$   & function, depending on the activation of the 
                 unit and the teaching input\\ 
  $h(\dots)$   & function, depending on the output of the
                 preceding element and the current\\ 
               & weight of the link\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\delta_{j}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9106: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$o_{j}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9108: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$t_{j}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9110: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$o_{i}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9114: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$w_{ij}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9118: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\Delta w_{ij}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9120: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\Delta w_{ij} & = & \eta \  \delta_{j} \  o_{i}\\ 
        \delta_{j}    & = & \left\{
        \begin{array}{ll}
        f'_{j}(net_{j})(t_{j} - o_{j}) &
        \mbox{if unit $j$ is a output-unit}\\ 
        f'_{j}(net_{j}) \sum_{k} \delta_{k} w_{jk} & 
         \mbox{if unit $j$ is a hidden-unit}\\ 
        \end{array}
        \right. 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
        $\eta$        & learning factor eta (a constant)\\ 
        $\delta_{j}$  & error (difference between the real output and
                        the teaching input) of unit $j$\\ 
        $t_{j}$       & teaching input of unit $j$\\ 
        $o_{i}$       & output of the preceding unit $i$\\ 
        $i$           & index of a predecessor to the current unit $j$
                        with link $w_{ij}$ from $i$ to $j$\\ 
        $j$           & index of the current unit\\ 
        $k$           & index of a successor to the current unit $j$
                        with link $w_{jk}$ from $j$ to $k$\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

\clearpage

OPTIONS}
}

}

}

}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{rcl}
      $n$&=&Number of observations (sample size)\\  
      $p$&=&Number of parameters, to be estimated (i.e. weights)\\  
      $SSE$&=&The sum of squared errors \\  
      $TSS$&=&The total sum of squares corrected for the mean \\  
             &&for the dependent variable\\  
  \end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$R^2$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9380: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$R^2 := 1- \frac{SSE}{TSS}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9382: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ MSE :=
  \frac{SSE}{n-p}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9384: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ RMSE := \sqrt{MSE}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9386: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$R^2_{adj}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9388: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$R^2$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9390: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ R^2_{adj} := 1-
    \frac{n-1}{n-p}\cdot (1-R^2) $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9392: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$R^2_{adj}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9394: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ PC := 1- \frac{n+p}{n-p}\cdot
    (1-R^2) $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9396: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$J_p$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9398: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ J_p:= (n+p)\cdot MSE/n $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9400: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ CMSEP:=\frac{SSE}{n-2p}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9402: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$  GCV:=\frac{SSE\cdot n}{(n-p)^2}
  $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9406: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ GMSEP:=\frac{MSE(n+1)(n-2)}{n(n-p-1)}
  $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9408: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$SHIBATA:=\frac{SSE(n+2p)}{n}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9410: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ AIC:=n\cdot \ln{\frac{SSE}{n}} + 2p $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9412: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$ SBC:=n\cdot \ln{\frac{SSE}{n}} + n
  \cdot \ln{p} $}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline9414: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage

\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\Delta w_{ij}(t+1) & = & \eta \  \delta_{j} \  o_{i} 
                                + \mu \ \Delta w_{ij}(t) \\ 
        \delta_{j}    & = & \left\{
        \begin{array}{ll}
        (f'_{j}(net_{j}) + c) (t_{j} - o_{j}) &
        \mbox{if unit $j$ is a output-unit}\\ 
        (f'_{j}(net_{j}) + c) \sum_{k} \delta_{k} w_{jk} & 
         \mbox{if unit $j$ is a hidden-unit}\\ 
        \end{array}
        \right. \\ 
  \end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{|ll|}
\hline
selection       & all selected units\\ 
:               & now the last key of a command sequence is pressed\\ 
$[$unit$]$      & the raster cursor is placed on a unit\\ 
$[$empty$]$     & the raster cursor is placed on an empty position\\ 
default         & the default values are used\\ 
TARGET          & the TARGET unit field in the info panel must be set\\ 
LINK            & the LINK field in the info panel must be set\\ 
site-links      & only links to the current site in the info panel
play a role\\ 
site            & the current site in the info panel must be set\\ 
popup           & a popup menu appears to ask for a value\\ 
site-popup      & if there are sites defined in the network, a popup\\ 
                & appears to choose the site for the operation\\ 
dest?           & a raster position for a destination must be clicked\\ 
                & with the mouse (e.g.\ in {\tt Units Move})\\ 
\hline
\end{tabular}
}

}

\end{displaymath}
}

{\tt eta} & The step size for changing the activations. It should
range\\  & from 1.0 to 10.0. Corresponds to the learning
factor in\\  & backpropagation.\\ 

{\tt delta\_max} & The maximum activation deviation of an
output unit.\\  & Units with higher deviation are called error units.\\ 
& A typical value of delta\_max is 0.1.\\ 

{\tt Input pattern}
                 & Initial activation of all input units.\\ 
{\tt 2nd approx ratio}
                 & Influence of the second approximation. Good values
range\\  & from 0.2 to 0.8.\\

\stepcounter{chapter}
\stepcounter{section}
\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\Delta w_{ij} & = & \eta \  \delta_{j} \  o_{i}\\ 
        \delta_{j}    & = & \left\{
        \begin{array}{ll}
        f'_{j}(net_{j})(t_{j} - o_{j}) &
        \mbox{if unit $j$ is an output unit}\\ 
        f'_{j}(net_{j}) \sum_{k} \delta_{k} w_{jk} & 
         \mbox{if unit $j$ is a hidden unit}\\ 
        \end{array}
        \right. \\ 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}% latex2html id marker 3733
\Delta w_{ij}(t+1) = 
        \eta * \delta_{j} * o_{i} + \alpha \Delta w_{ij}(t) 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{% latex2html id marker 10993
$\alpha$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline10993: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\Delta w_{ij} (t + 1) = \eta\, \delta_j\, o_i - d\, w_{ij}(t)
\end{displaymath}
}

\stepcounter{section}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\Delta(t+1) w_{ij} = \frac{S(t+1)}{S(t)-S(t+1)} \Delta(t) w_{ij} 
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $w_{ij}$      & weight between units i and j\\ 
  $\Delta(t+1)$ & actual weight change\\ 
  $S(t+1)$      & partial derivative of the error function by $w_{ij}$\\ 
  $S(t)$        & the last partial derivative\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

\stepcounter{section}
\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{% latex2html id marker 11016
$\alpha$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11016: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}% latex2html id marker 11014
E = \sum (t_i - o_i)^2 + 10^{-\alpha} \sum w_{ij}^2 \end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{% latex2html id marker 11018
$\alpha$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11018: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{% latex2html id marker 11020
$\alpha = 4$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11020: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$1:10000 (1:10^4)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11022: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\triangle_{ij}^{(t)}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11026: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray}\label{man} \triangle w_{ij}^{(t)} = \left\{ \begin{array}{r@{\quad,\quad}l}
                    -\triangle_{ij}^{(t)} & \mbox{if } \frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t)} > 0
   \\ 
                    +\triangle_{ij}^{(t)} & \mbox{if } \frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t)} < 0
   \\ 
                    0 & \mbox{else }
                   \end{array} \right.
\end{eqnarray}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t)}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11028: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\triangle_{ij}(t)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11030: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage

}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\triangle_{ij}(t)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11034: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray}\triangle_{ij}^{(t)} = \left\{ \begin{array}{r@{\quad,\quad}l} \eta^+ *
\triangle_{ij}^{(t-1)} & \mbox{if } \frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t-1)} * \frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t)} >0 \\ 
\eta^- * \triangle_{ij}^{(t-1)} & \mbox{if } \frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t-1)} *
\frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{ij}}^{(t)} <0 \\  \triangle_{ij}^{(t-1)} & \mbox{else} \end{array}
\right.
\end{eqnarray}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\mbox{where } 0 < \eta^- < 1 < \eta^+ \end{displaymath}
}

\stepcounter{subsubsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}E=\sum_{p}\frac{1}{2}\sum_{o}(y_{po}-t_{po})^{2}
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$t_{po}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11299: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$y_{po}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11301: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}e_{po} & = & (y_{po}-t_{po})f_{p}'(net_{o})  \nonumber      \\ 
\frac{\partial E}{\partial w_{io}} & = & \sum_{p}e_{po} I_{ip}, 
\label{formel}
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$f'_{p}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11309: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$I_{ip}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11313: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$w_{io}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11319: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$y_{po}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11327: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$e_{po}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11329: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}C & =
&\sum_{o}\left|\sum_{p}(y_{po} - \bar{y_{o}})(e_{po} - \bar{e_{o}})\right| \nonumber \\ 
  & = &\sum_{o}\left|
        \sum_{p} y_{po} e_{po} - 
\bar{e_{o}}\sum_{p}y_{po} \right| \nonumber \\ 
  & = &\sum_{o}\left| \sum_{p} y_{po} (e_{po}- \bar{e_{o}}) \right|,
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\bar{y_{o}}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11331: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\bar{e_{o}}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11333: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\delta_{p} & = &\sum_{o}\sigma_{o}(e_{po} - \bar{e_{j}})f_{p}' \nonumber \\ 
\frac{\partial C}{\partial w_{i}} & = & \sum_{p} \delta_{p}I_{pi},
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\sigma_{o}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11337: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

\stepcounter{subsubsection}

\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}V(t) & = & f_{act}\left(\sum_{i} I_{i}(t)w_{ir} + V(t-1)w_{s} \right),
\label{recact}
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
$f_{act}$: & any activation function \\ 
$w_{ir}$:  & the weight of the connection between the input unit $i$ and 
             the unit $r$ \\ 
$w_{s}$:   & the value of the self recurrent connection. \\ 
$I_{i}$:   & output of the input unit $i$ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$V(t)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11362: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$w_{ir}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11364: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$w_{s}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline11366: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\frac{\partial V}{\partial w_{ir}} & = 
   f'_{act}(t) (I_{i}(t)+w_{s} \frac{\partial V(t-1)}{\partial w_{ir}}) 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\frac{\partial V}{\partial w_{s}} & = 
   f'_{act}(t) (V(t-1)+w_{s} \frac{\partial V(t-1)}{\partial w_{s}}).
\end{eqnarray*}
}

G_\Box = \left( \begin{array}{ccc}

\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$W_i$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12343: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$(x_i)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12345: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$W_j$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12347: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$(w_{ij})$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12349: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}Net_j(t) = X \cdot W_j = \sum_{i=1}^n x_i(t) w_{ij}(t) \end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$W_c$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12355: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}Net_c(t) = \max_j \{ Net_j(t) \} = X\cdot W_c\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$W_c$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12363: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$N(1)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12365: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$N(2)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12367: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$N(1)$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12369: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$N_c$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12371: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray*}\Delta w_{ij}(t) & = & e_j(t)\cdot(x_i(t)-w_{ij}(t)) }\hfill{ \hbox{~~for}\:j\in N_c\\  
\Delta w_{ij}(t) & = & 0 }\hfill{ \hbox{~~for}\:j \not\in N_c\\ 
\end{eqnarray*}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$e_j(t) = h(t) \cdot e^{-(d_j/r(t))^2}$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12373: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
$d_j$:     & distance between $W_j$ and winner $W_c$\\ 
$h(t)$:    & adaptation height at time t with $0\leq h(t)\leq 1$\\ 
$r(t)$:    & radius of the spatial neighborhood $N_c$ at time t\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\Delta w_{ij} = n * input_i * input_j
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $n$           &is the learning rate\\ 
  $input_i$     &is the external input to node i\\ 
  $input_j$     &is the external input to node j\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{displaymath}\Delta w_{ij} = n * d_i * a_j
\end{displaymath}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $n$           &is the learning rate\\ 
  $a_j$         &is the activation of the source node\\ 
  $d_i$         &is the error in the destination node.\\ 
                &This error is defined as the external input - the
                internal input.\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{ll}
  $neti$        &is the net input to i (external + internal)\\ 
  $E$           &is the excitation parameter (here set to 0.15)\\ 
  $D$           &is the decay parameter (here set to 0.15)\\ 
\end{tabular}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$7/4$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12582: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

\stepcounter{chapter}
\stepcounter{section}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{figure}[htbp]
\mbox{\ }\hrulefill\mbox{\ }

\begin{enumerate}
\item Choose a reasonable network architecture.
\item Train the net with backpropagation or any similar learning
function into a minimum of the network.
\item Compute the {\it saliency} (relevance for the performance of the
network) of each element (link or unit respectively).
\item Prune the element with the smallest saliency.
\item Retrain the net (into a minimum again).
\item If the net error is not too big, repeat the procedure from
step~3 on.
\item (optional) Recreate the last pruned element in order to achieve
a small net error again.
\end{enumerate}

\mbox{\ }\hrulefill\mbox{\ }


\label{prsensalg}
\end{figure}
}

\stepcounter{section}
\stepcounter{subsection}
\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray}\delta E & = & E (W + \delta W) - E (W) \nonumber \\ 
 & = & \frac{\partial E(W)}{\partial W} \cdot \delta W +
\frac{1}{2} \frac{\partial^2 E(W)}{\partial W^2} +
R_2 (W + \delta W)
\label{prtaylorobd}
\end{eqnarray}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{equation}\delta E\ =\ \frac{1}{2} \sum_{1 \leq i,j \leq n_n} h_{(ij),(ij)} 
\cdot \delta w_{ij}^2
\end{equation}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{equation}\delta E\ =\ \frac{1}{2}\ \delta W^T \cdot H \cdot \delta W
\label{probstaylorred}
\end{equation}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{equation}\min_{(ij)}\ \left\{\ 
\min_{\delta W}\ 
\left(\frac{1}{2}\ \delta W^T \cdot H \cdot \delta W \right)
\ |\ E_{(ij)}^T \cdot \delta W\ +\ w_{ij}\ =\ 0 
\ \right\}
\end{equation}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{equation}L\ =\ \frac{1}{2}\ \delta W^T \cdot H \cdot \delta W
\ +\ \lambda\cdot (E_{(ij)}^T \cdot \delta W\ +\ w_{(ij)})
\end{equation}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \setbox\sizebox=\hbox{$\lambda$}\lthtmltypeout{latex2htmlSize :tex2html_wrap_inline12590: \the\ht\sizebox::\the\dp\sizebox.}\box\sizebox
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{eqnarray}\delta W & = & - \frac{w_{ij}}{[H^{-1}]_{(ij),(ij)}} \cdot H^{-1} \cdot
E_{(ij)} \label{obsgewver} \\ 
L_{(ij)} & = & s_{ij}\ =\ \frac{1}{2} \frac{w_{(ij)}^2}{[H^{-1}]_{(ij),(ij)}}
\mbox{\rule{0mm}{8mm}}
\end{eqnarray}
}

\stepcounter{subsection}
{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{|llp{68mm}|}
\hline
Key             &Value          &Meaning\\  \hline \hline

InitFunction:   &$<$string$>$           &Name of the initialization
function.\\ 

InitParam:      &$<$float$>$ $\cdots$ &'NoOfInitParam' pa\-ra\-meters
for in\-i\-tiali\-zation function, separated by blanks.\\ 

LearnParam:     &$<$float$>$ $\cdots$ &'NoOfLearnParam' parameters
for learning function, separated by blanks.\\ 

UpdateParam:    &$<$float$>$ $\cdots$ &'NoOfUpdateParam' parameters
for the update function, separated by blanks.\\ 

LearnPatternFile: &$<$string$>$         &Filename of the learning patterns.\\ 

MaxErrorToStop: &$<$float$>$        &Network error when learning is to
be halted.\\ 

MaxLearnCycles: &$<$int$>$              &Maximum number of learning
cycles to be executed.\\ 

NetworkFile:    &$<$string$>$    &Filename of the net to be trained.\\ 

NoOfInitParam:  &$<$int$>$ &Number of parameters for the initialization
function.\\  

NoOfLearnParam: &$<$int$>$ &No of parameters for learning function.\\ 

NoOfUpdateParam: &$<$int$>$ &No of parameters for update function.\\ 

NoOfVarDim:     &$<$int$>$ $<$int$>$   &Number of variable dimensions of
the input and output patterns.\\ 

PerformActions:         &none &Execution run separator.\\ 
\hline
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{|llp{70mm}|}
\hline
Key             &Value          &Meaning\\  \hline \hline

PruningMaxRetrainCycles: & $<$int$>$ & maximum no. of cycles per
retraining \\ 

PruningMaxErrorIncrease: & $<$float$>$ & Percentage to be added to the
first net error. The resulting value cannot be exceeded by the net
error, unless it is lower than the accepted error \\ 

PruningAcceptedError: & $<$float$>$ & Maximum accepted error.\\ 

PruningRecreate: & [ YES $|$ NO ] & Flag for reestablishing the last
state of the net at the end of pruning \\ 

PruningOBSInitParam: & $<$float$>$ & initial value for OBS \\ 

PruningInputPruning: & [ YES $|$ NO ] & Flag for input unit pruning \\ 

PruningHiddenPruning: & [ YES $|$ NO ] & Flag for hidden unit pruning \\ 

ResultFile:     &$<$string$>$           &Filename of the result file.\\ 

ResultIncludeInput:     &[ YES $|$ NO ]         &Flag for inclusion of input
patterns in the result file.\\ 

ResultIncludeOutput:    &[ YES $|$ NO ]         &Flag for inclusion of output
learning patterns in the result file.\\  

SubPatternOSize: &$<$int$>$ $\cdots$ &NoOfVarDim[2] int values that specify
the shape of the sub patterns of each output pattern.\\ 

SubPatternOStep: &$<$int$>$ $\cdots$ &NoOfVarDim[2] int values that
specify the shifting steps for the sub patterns of each output pattern.\\ 

TestPatternFile: &$<$string$>$          &Filename of the test patterns.\\ 

TrainedNetworkFile:   &$<$string$>$     & Filename where the net should
be stored after training / initialization.\\ 

Type:           &$<$string$>$   &The type of grammar that corresponds
to this file. Valid types are:\\  & &'SNNSBATCH\_1': performs only one
execution run.\\  & &'SNNSBATCH\_2': performs multiple execution runs.\\  

ResultMinMaxPattern:    &$<$int$>$ $<$int$>$ &Number of the first and
last pattern to be used for result file generation.\\ 

Shuffle:        &[ YES $|$ NO ]         &Flag for pattern shuffling.\\ 

ShuffleSubPat:  &[ YES $|$ NO ]         &Flag for subpattern shuffling.\\ 

SubPatternISize: &$<$int$>$ $\cdots$ &NoOfVarDim[1] int values that specify
the shape of the sub patterns of each input pattern.\\ 

SubPatternIStep: &$<$int$>$ $\cdots$ &NoOfVarDim[1] int values that
specify the shifting steps for the sub patterns of each input pattern.\\ 
\hline
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{|l|p{90mm}|}
\hline

missing key             &resulting behavior\\  \hline \hline

InitFunction:           &The net is not initialized.\\ 

InitParam:              &Init function gets only zero values as parameters.\\  

LearnParam:             &Learning function gets only zero values as
                        parameters.\\  

UpdateParam:            &Update function gets only zero values as
                        parameters.\\  

LearnPatternFile:       &Abort with error message if more than 0
                        learning cycles are specified. Initialization
                        can be performed if init function does not
                        require patterns.\\ 

MaxErrorToStop:         &Training runs for 'MaxLearnCycles:' cycles.\\ 

MaxLearnCycles:         &No training takes place. If training is
supposed to run until {\sl MaxErrorToStop}, a rather huge number
should be supplied here. (skipping this entry would inhibit training
completely).\\   

NetworkFile:            &Abort with error message.\\ 

NoOfInitParam:          &No parameters are assigned to the
                        initialization function. 
                        Error message from the SNNS kernel possible.\\ 
NoOfLearnParam:         &No parameters are assigned to the learning function.
                        Error message from the SNNS kernel possible.\\ 

NoOfUpdateParam:        &No parameters are assigned to the update function.\\ 

NoOfVarDim:             &Network can not handle variable pattern sizes.\\ 
PerformActions:         &Only one execution run is performed. Repeated
keywords lead to deletion of older values.\\ 

ResultFile:             &No result file is generated.\\ 

ResultIncludeInput:     &The result file does NOT contain input Patterns.\\ 

ResultIncludeOutput:    &The result file DOES contain learn output
Patterns.\\ 

ResultMinMaxPattern:    &All patterns are propagated.\\ 

Shuffle:                &Patterns are not shuffled.\\ 

ShuffleSubPat:          &Subpatterns are not shuffled.\\ 

SubPatternISize:        & \\ 
SubPatternIStep:        & \\ 
SubPatternOSize:        & \\ 
SubPatternOStep:        &Abort with error message if `NoOfVarDim:' was
specified.\\  \hline
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}{|l|p{105mm}|}
\hline
missing key            &resulting behavior\\  \hline \hline

TestPatternFile:        &Result file generation uses the learning patterns.
                        If they are not specified either, the program
                        is aborted with an error message when trying
                        to generate a result  file.\\ 

TrainedNetworkFile:     &Network is not saved after training /
                        initialization. It is used for
                        result file generation.\\  

Type:                   &Abort with error message.\\ 
\hline
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{table}[h]
\begin{center}
\begin{tabular}{|l|l|r|}     \hline
{\bf Function} & {\bf Formula}\\  \hline \hline
{\tt Linear}      & $net_j(t) = \sum_{i} {w_{ij}o_i}$ \\  \hline
{\tt Produkt}     & $net_j(t) = \prod_{i} {w_{ij}o_i}$ \\  \hline
{\tt PI}          & $net_j(t) = \prod_{i} {o_i}$ \\  \hline
{\tt Max}         & $net_j(t) = \max_{i} {w_{ij}o_i}$ \\  \hline
{\tt Min}         & $net_j(t) = \min_{i} {w_{ij}o_i}$ \\  \hline
{\tt At\_least\_2}  & $net_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                                     0 & \mbox{if } \sum_{i} {w_{ij}o_i} < 2 \\ 
                                     1 & \mbox{else.}
                                  \end{array}
                          \right.$ \\  \hline
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
\end{table}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{tabular}% latex2html id marker 8441
{|l|l|}     \hline
{\bf Function} & {\bf Formula}\\  \hline \hline
{\tt BAM} & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               1  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) > 0$}\\ 
                               a_j(t-1)  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) = 0$}\\ 
                               -1 & \mbox{if $net_j(t) < 0$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt BSB} & $a_j(t) = net_j(t) * \theta_j$  \\  \hline
{\tt Elliott} & $a_j(t) = \frac{net_j(t) + \theta_j}{1 + |net_j(t)+ \theta_j|}$ \\  \hline
{\tt Identity}   & $a_j(t) = net_j(t)$ \\  \hline
{\tt IdentityPlusBias}   & $a_j(t) = net_j(t) + \theta_j $ \\  \hline
{\tt Logistic}   & $a_j(t) = \frac{1}{1 + e^{-(net_j(t) + \theta_j)}}$ \\  \hline
{\tt Logistic\_notInhibit}   & like Logistic, but skip input from units\\ 
                   & named "Inhibit"\\  \hline
{\tt Logistic\_Tbl}   & like Logistic, but with table lookup \\  
                  & instead of computation \\  \hline
{\tt MinOutPlusWeight} & $a_j(t) = \min_{i} (w_{ij} + o_i)$ \\  \hline
{\tt Perceptron} & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               1  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) \geq \theta_j$}\\ 
                               0  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) < \theta_j$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt Produc}t & $a_j(t) = \prod_i w_{ij}o_i$ \\  \hline
{\tt RBF\_Gaussian} & see the chapter about RBFs in the user manual \\  \hline
{\tt RBF\_MultiQuadratic} & see the chapter about RBFs in the user manual \\  \hline
{\tt RBF\_ThinPlateSpline} & see the chapter about RBFs in the user manual \\  \hline
{\tt RM}         & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                        0.85 * a_j(t-1) +\\  
                        \vspace{1ex}
                        0.15*net_j(t)*\\  (1-a_j(t-1))& \mbox{if $net_j(t) > 0$}\\ 
                        0.85 * a_j(t-1) + \\ 
                        0.15*net_j(t)*\\  (1+a_j(t-1))& \mbox{if $net_j(t) \leq0$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt Signum}     & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               1  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) > 0$}\\ 
                               -1 & \mbox{if $net_j(t) \leq 0$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt Signum0}    & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               1  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) > 0$}\\ 
                               0  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) = 0$}\\ 
                               -1 & \mbox{if $net_j(t) < 0$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt StepFunc}   & $a_j(t) = \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               1  & \mbox{if $net_j(t) > 0$}\\ 
                               0 & \mbox{if $net_j(t) \leq 0$}\\ 
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt TanH} & $a_j(t) = \tanh(net_j(t) + \theta_j)$ \\  \hline
{\tt TanH\_Xdiv2} & $a_j(t) = \tanh(net_j(t)/2 + \theta_j)$ \\  \hline
\end{tabular}
}

{\newpage
\clearpage
\samepage \begin{table}[htbp]
\begin{center}
\begin{tabular}{|l|l|r|}     \hline
{\bf Function} & {\bf Formula}\\  \hline \hline
{\tt Identity} & $o_j(t)=a_j(t)$ \\  \hline
{\tt Clip\_0\_1}    & $o_j(t)= \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               0      & \mbox{if $a_j(t) \leq 0$}\\ 
                               a_j(t) & \mbox{if $a_j(t) < 1$}\\ 
                               1 & \mbox{if $a_j(t) \geq 1$}
                        \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt Clip\_1\_1}    & $o_j(t)= \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               -1     & \mbox{if $a_j(t) \leq -1$}\\ 
                               a_j(t) & \mbox{if $-1 < a_j(t) < 1$}\\ 
                               1      & \mbox{if $a_j(t) \geq 1$}
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
{\tt Threshold\_0.5} & $o_j(t)=  \left\{ \begin{array}{ll}
                               0      & \mbox{if $a_j(t) \leq 0.5$}\\ 
                               1      & \mbox{if $a_j(t) > 0.5$}
                               \end{array}
                       \right. $\\  \hline
\end{tabular}
\end{center}
\end{table}
}
